# test(gemm): cover block-scale public graph replay and scale edges

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/NVIDIA/cudnn-frontend/pull/1259) · state: closed · merged: True

## Original PR description

## Before submitting

- [x] I agree to license this contribution under the terms of LICENSE.txt.
- [x] I ran `pre-commit run --files test/python/gemm/frost/test_block_scale_graph_replay.py` (all applicable hooks passed).
- [x] I reviewed the root and test AGENTS.md rules. The test saves/restores the exact fixture handle stream and explicitly resets its CUDA Graph in finally.
- [ ] Requested labels: cat-ci, area:frost, orig-external. GitHub rejected the label update with an updatePullRequest permission error; the PR was created successfully. A maintainer needs to apply these labels.

## Summary

Add L1 coverage of the public FROST block-scale graph execution path:

- NVFP4, MXFP4 and MXFP8 at 128×128×128 and the 144×144×256 tail shape;
- NVFP4 zero scales, mixed zero scales, and finite maximum scales / positive FP4 inputs whose FP16 output overflows to +inf;
- capture once, then replay with original, zeroed, repeated-zero and restored input bytes at stable addresses;
- poison the output before every replay and compare against the independent dequantized reference (FP64 for the explicit edge patterns).

The test explicitly selects and asserts `frost_gemm`; another engine cannot silently satisfy it. It uses the existing graph/input/reference helpers and existing supported-family marker. No production kernel, support gate, heuristic or public API changes.

## Why

Existing numeric tests do not by themselves ensure that captured **public graph execution** observes live packed input storage at these scale boundaries. This adds that contract coverage. It is not presented as a new Thor enablement fix or a performance optimization.

## Testing

Base: `804ff02266a8db5080b1928fc5d0abe1129686d9`.

Physical Jetson AGX Thor (CC 11.0), CUDA 13.2 / nvcc 13.2.78, driver 595.78, Python 3.12, PyTorch 2.13.0+cu130, cuDNN backend 9.20, CuTe DSL 4.7.1. Used a private frontend source build at the above SHA; the existing model-serving environment was not modified. The conftest banner confirms the private package import path.

From `test/python`:

```sh
python -m pytest -o addopts= -m L1 -q gemm/frost/test_block_scale_graph_replay.py
```

- First process: **12 passed** (48 graph replays).
- Fresh process with fresh private JIT/cache paths: **12 passed** (48 graph replays).
- Negative control: temporarily suppressing `capture.replay()` fails the first test because the poisoned output remains NaN; restored the original file before the final passing run. This tests the detector, not an alleged production bug.
- Pre-commit: black, black-jupyter and SPDX checks passed; C++ formatting not applicable.

No SM100/SM103/SM107 hardware runtime, cross-Toolkit, performance or model-E2E claim is made. The capability marker follows the existing SM100-family test contract; Thor is the hardware actually exercised here.

## Related issues

No existing cuDNN issue was identified for this specific coverage addition.

## Repository merge metadata

The upstream `pre-commit` check passed. The `merge-requirements` check reports missing **Milestone** and **Project board** assignments. Maintainers need to assign those repository fields and the requested labels; the external author does not have label-management permission. No milestone or project assignment is claimed.
